- **Not covered:** the embedded lane still itemises from anatomy rows (a remainder can show there); the CONTEXT WINDOW bar and THIS CALL still read the anatomy rows, so on cc-bridge they keep the old breakdown (and the moral code at 0, F6) until they read the per-call composition too.

### FIXED [gate-blindspot]: the moral code reached the claude CLI workers as a 2 KB preview, not as the pack (2026-10-02; bug id `moral-code-reaches-cli-as-preview`)

- **Found by:** the per-call itemisation above (`cli-context.ts` counts hook context as the CLI sends it). The moral-code bucket measures ≈480 tokens per call where the pack is ≈11.5k.
- **Known (measured, the four newest worker transcripts of 2026-10-02):** the only copy of the pack in what the CLI sends is the SessionStart `hook_additional_context`: 5,367 chars, carrying the opening `<moral_code source="tinkerclaw">` tag and NO closing tag, in the CLI's "output too large, saved to a file" preview form. No copy in the `prompt_snapshot` system prompt, none in any user message. The full 40,329-char pack is only in the hook's stdout, which the CLI never sends (CLI 2.1.287: `hook_success.stdout` is not sent; additionalContext over 10,000 chars is replaced by a ~2,000-char preview plus a file path). The same cap shows in an interactive Claude Code session: its SessionStart output was shown as "Output too large (39.4KB) … Preview (first 2KB)".
- **Why nothing caught it (code):** since 2026-09-22 `moral-code-delivery.ts` hands new conversations the pack through the `tinkerclaw-core` plugin's SessionStart hook instead of the system prompt; the fallback (prefix the next user message once) fires only when `transcriptHasMoralCode` finds no marker, and the marker IS in the transcript — in the truncated preview and in the unsent stdout. The test passes on a pack the model never received.
- **Don't know:** since when the CLI caps hook context at 10,000 chars, and whether a worker ever reads the saved file on its own.
- **Decision (the owner, 2026-10-02):** _"Fix the moral code to be injected, in full, at the creation and after compaction"_ — option (b), the hook kept, the pack split.
- **Fix:** `4443943a251`. `claude-plugins/tinkerclaw-core`: `hooks.json` registers 10 SessionStart slots (startup | clear | compact), `moral-code-session-start.mjs --part i` prints part i (`moral-code-parts.mjs`): a complete block — the published marker, `[moral code, part i of N]`, the pack's text, the closing tag — under 9,500 chars, cut before a heading; the parts' texts in order are the pack byte for byte. The published pack (40,329 chars) goes out as 5 parts of 7.3k–9.1k. Dedupe per session + source + part (the plugin loads twice in a worker). Version 0.2.0, so `claude plugin update` refreshes the marketplace copy every session also loads. The resume check (`contract.ts` `moralCodeDeliveredIn`) counts only text the CLI sends, after the last compaction, and only a complete delivery (one unlabelled block, or all parts of one set): the preview and the unsent stdout no longer pass, so a resumed worker that only ever saw the preview gets the whole pack prefixed to its next message. Rules: `tool-loop.md` (2026-10-02 paragraph).
- **Tests:** `src/moral-code/delivery-parts.test.ts` (runs every hooks.json command as the CLI does: every output under 10,000 chars, complete labelled blocks, byte-for-byte reassembly, silent spare slots, a huge section cut at paragraphs and lines, dedupe across two plugin copies, and the parts — shuffled — passing the resume check while a preview, a missing part or stdout does not), `contract.test.ts` (sent text only, after the last compaction, no sidechain), `pack.test.ts` and `moral-code-delivery.test.ts` rewritten to the new contract. Red first: 6 of 9 failed before the fix.
- **Live check:** the call timeline's per-call moral-code bucket (`cli-context.ts` counts every complete block) reads ≈11.5k on a session started after the fix, where it read ≈480.

- **Tests:** `src/gateway/chat-history-archive.test.ts`, `src/gateway/server-methods/chat.reset-archive.test.ts` (real handler, real store, the import merge through the bridge map), `tinker-ui/src/history-paging.test.ts`, `tinker-ui/src/chat-units.test.ts`. Real browser: `tinker-ui/e2e/chat-viewport/` check 7 (develop's build fails it).

### FIXED [mechanism-starved]: Thalamus kept routing Auto turns to Grok, whose every call fails with 426, and the second turn died (2026-10-02; bug id `thalamus-426-not-a-failover`)

- **Symptom:** _"Strange, Luna is picking up the prompt now, and the Thalamus panel says Grok is answering instead."_ The next turn in the same chat ended with `426 "Your Grok CLI version (0.2.91) is outdated. Please update to version 1.0.13 or later"`, and no answer.
- **Measured (gateway journal, chat `mtba8duf`):** with the dial on default and every suggestion a Claude model, Thalamus chose `xai/grok-4.7` at 17:07 and 17:09. At 17:07 Grok failed in 2 s and the turn reached `openai-codex/gpt-5.6-luna` only through an unrelated live model switch (`candidate_failed … reason=unknown … detail=Live session model switch requested`). At 17:09 there was no such switch: the run ended `isError=true` and no fallback decision was logged.
- **Root cause:** `classifyFailoverClassificationFromHttpStatus` had no case for 426, so the error classified as null, which is "not a failover": `run.ts` rethrew it and the chain never moved. And `recordSupplyLimit` cools only `rate_limit` / `billing`, so xAI was never cooled and every next plan picked Grok again. The two halves of the full deploy's "a limit reroutes" (§5.8AE) were both starved by one unmapped status.
- **Fix (`82bcf23dc6d`):** a 426 classifies as `auth` (the provider refuses this client; auth cools its profile and model-fallback skips its other models, then probes later, so it heals once the client is fixed). The cooling store cools the supply on a 426 as `client_rejected`, default 30 minutes, from the status or the error text; a plain 401 still does not cool. `model-fallback.ts` passes the status through. Stop-gap until the build ran: xAI cooled by hand in `~/.openclaw/thalamus-cooling.json` until 2026-10-03 05:19 (0 xAI routes and 0 426s after it).
- **Tests:** `model-fallback.test.ts` "a 426 … fails over instead of ending the turn, and cools the supply" (red against develop, green with the fix); `thalamus-cooling.test.ts` (426 cools by status and by text; 401 does not); `classifyfailoverreason.test.ts` (426 → auth).
- **Not fixed here:** Grok itself. The client still reports 0.2.91 to a proxy that requires ≥ 1.0.13 (`reference_grok_cli_426_kills_the_cron_fleet`); raising it is the architect's call. This fix only makes Thalamus route around it.

### FIXED [id-reuse-across-restarts]: the Jev window vanished from chat turns after a gateway restart, because every restart gave a tab's next turn the id "#1" again (2026-10-02; bug id `amygdala-turn-id-reuse`)

- **Symptom:** the architect, 18:16: the Jev chat summary was "not showing anymore here".
- **Measured:** `amygdala.sqlite` for the CTO tab: 3 prompt steps (12:26, 12:43, 18:17), each the first after a restart, all with turn id `agent:main:tinker:mukvdku7#1`; the page placed that merged turn once, under the first message, so later replies had no window.
- **Root cause (code):** `SessionTracker.turnN` in `extensions/tinkerclaw-amygdala/src/session-tracker.ts` lives in process memory and starts at 0 in each gateway process.
- **Fix:** `8e6836f20a9`: the tracker takes an epoch (the process start, from `runtime.ts`) and ids become `<tab>#<epoch>.<n>`. Turns already stored stay merged. Test in `session-tracker.test.ts`.
- **Same change set:** `fd9bc08399e` reworked the window so the would-be changes come first with votes in view (the architect: "I cannot vote anything ... there are too many things").

### FIXED [scope-mismatch]: the ↻ buttons' status poll reloaded every open Tinker page when ANY rebuild ended, mid-reply, and nothing recorded who caused a reload (2026-10-03; bug id `rebuild-poll-reloads-every-page`)

- **Symptom:** the owner, 2026-10-03: "The tinker ui is refreshing all the time. I wish we had a way to trace who triggers it and why, and could learn when not to do it. It is annoying, and a gateway restart takes a few minutes to recover, cutting the flow of my work."
- **Measured (gateway and tinker-prod-ui journals, freeze-thaw history, 7 days to 10-03 06:01):** gateway restarts per day 0–2 until 09-29, then 18 / 13 / 17 on 09-30 / 10-01 / 10-02, the days restarts became agents' to make and builds started being pushed to open pages. In the last 48 h: 29 restarts asked for by 11 different chats, terminals and a cron (10 in 3 h 16 min on 10-02, from 5 of them), 43 page reloads of which 15 matched no trigger, and 15 of the 46 restart records named no chat (every restart a terminal session made). Median SIGTERM → `[gateway] ready` 56.5 s; SIGTERM → the skill's "healthy" 125 s (worst 258 s). The 5-minute restart guard refused twice; the agents waited it out.
- **Root cause:** `bindRebuildButton`'s status poll called `location.reload()` when a run reported `ok`. Since 2026-09-30 its 15 s check attaches every open page to runs an AGENT starts (skill `tinker-rebuild`), so every page reloaded at the end of every full rebuild, at once, bypassing `requestUiReload`'s turn and composer rules. Two more silent paths hid it: the deferred reload's drain reloaded without a word, and no page load left a server-side trace, so afterwards a reload could not be told from a hand F5. The scope mismatch: a per-page follower performed a page-wide side effect for a run it did not own.
- **Fix:** `52bedc12c24` (the page reloads itself only through `reloadPage`, which records the cause and posts it on the next load to `POST /api/page-load`, with a toast; the poll reloads only the page whose click started the run, onto a different bundle), `eba214056ff` (tinker-prod-ui's disruption ledger `~/.local/state/tinkerclaw/disruptions.jsonl`: rebuild start and end, build-push with its cause, page-load; `--requester` through `rebuild-and-restart.sh`), `25efa96a595` (the first reload's cause wins: a poll firing during the unload relabelled an "after-turn" reload "immediate"), `89f1d8a67f4` (`.toast` had no CSS, so all 15 `showToast` messages landed out of sight). Host side, in the private skills: `freeze_thaw.py restart` resolves a readable requester (Tinker tab, or terminal Claude session name) before it detaches and writes `gateway-restart` / `gateway-restart-refused` lines; `tinker_rebuild.py` sends the requester, takes `frontend --reason`, and `why` prints the ledger. Rules: `lifecycles.md` L4b "Every reload and every restart is on the record".
- **Tests:** Chromium against a sandbox tinker-prod-ui (temp HOME and state, the chat-viewport mock gateway), an agent's frontend rebuild ending while a reply streams. Develop's build: reloaded mid-reply (red), and the next load could only call it "manual". This branch: 14/14, no reload during the reply, one reload 109 s later recorded `new-build` / `after-turn` with requester and reason, `opened` and `manual` loads told apart, toast seen on screen. Gate: `lifecycles.md` verify "L4b hot deploy" (develop had 4 bare `location.reload();`, this branch 1).
- **Not fixed here:** how often agents restart. The ledger names them; whether restarts should be batched or wait until the owner is idle is the owner's call. Restarts made by the gateway's own config watcher still carry no requester.

### FIXED [config-dead-code+unverified-state]: every spawned subagent ran on the default model, because the store's entry cap deleted the child's model pin in the save that wrote it (2026-10-03; bug id `subagent-model-pin-evicted`)

- **Symptom:** a spawn with `--model` returned `modelApplied:true`, yet the child ran on `agents.defaults.model.primary`. From 09-26 to 10-03, 51 of 51 subagent turns ran on the primary (llm-ledger), including pins to Haiku, Opus 5.5, gpt-6-astra and grok. The bug had been open since 2026-08-31.
- **Measured:** the live store held 239 protected conversation keys, with `session.maintenance.maxEntries` 80 in mode `enforce`. A probe's child key never appeared in `sessions.json` across 100 polls during its run, and the journal logged `capped session entry count` at each of the spawn's store writes (06:51:07 to 06:51:11).
- **Root cause (code):** `capEntryCount` (`src/config/sessions/store-maintenance.ts`) gave background entries `maxEntries - preservedCount` slots. `65de05e7437` made conversation keys unevictable, but they still counted. Once they alone reached `maxEntries`, the budget was zero, and every maintained save deleted every subagent and cron entry, including the one it had just written. `agent-command.ts` then found no `modelOverride`. `modelApplied` only reports that the write resolved; nothing reads the entry back.
- **Fix:** `8b44cd8c21a`. The newest `min(maxEntries, 25)` background entries always survive a cap. Conversation keys are still never evicted, and a store without them caps exactly as before. Tests in `store.pruning.test.ts` and `store.pruning.integration.test.ts` fail without the fix with `expected undefined to be 'claude-haiku-4-5'`.
- **Same day:** the primary moved to Opus 5.5 and every model-choice site moved to the top-ranked model of its line (`39c977c33e4`, `62a489fe22d`), so unpinned children were already on Opus 5.5 before this fix landed.

### FIXED [display-misclassify]: the gateway's restart resume drew as the architect's own prompt, because Thalamus advice rode in front of its [System] mark (2026-10-03; bug id `injected-prompt-shown-as-his`)

- **Symptom:** the architect, 2026-10-03 08:32: "Where does this previous prompt come from? Not from me for sure. If it was an agent that introduced it ... it should be properly presented as such, with the identity of the agent, and in blue." The message (2026-10-02 18:20, role user) was the gateway's restart resume.
- **Root cause (code):** `detectSystemNotice` (`tinker-ui/src/system-notice.ts`, the 2026-08-24 blue "Automated system message" design) matched only when `[System]` was the first characters. Thalamus's short list (`extensions/tinkerclaw-thalamus/src/shortlist-seam.ts`) had been prepended to the resume, with a delivery stamp after it, so the content-block render path fell through to his bubble. Census of the CTO tab's last 10 user rows: 4 machine-written, this one shown as his; producers: 7 that write `[System]`, plus `[injected: …]` and agents' `<cross-session-message>` (the latter never recognised).
- **Fix:** `01e7a17853d`: the notice reads past a stamp and an advice block, accepts `[System · name]`, and the badge names the sender ("Automated message from the gateway · restart recovery"); another agent's message is badged with its name; `[injected: name]` shows the name. `3405ac482bc`: Thalamus gives no list to such messages. Checked in Chromium on the built page with the real message.
- **For new producers:** keep `[System]` (tinker-bridge's cli-command checks for it) and name yourself as `[System · name]`, or use `[injected: name]`.

### FIXED [mixed-coordinates+chat-divergence+early-return-drops-attribute]: the live chat drew an answer twice, or the answer and then its own tail from a mid-word point, while a reload showed it once (2026-10-03; bug id `live-answer-twice-or-tail`)

- **Symptom:** the owner, 2026-10-03: _"The front-end of tinkerclaw is still duplicating some messages in the chat. It is strange also that if I refresh the page, the duplicate answer goes away."_
- **Measured (read-only):** the 28 per-tab snapshots written since 2026-10-01, each compared with what `chat.history` serves. 9 showed a repeated bubble. 6 were separate served records that a reload keeps (prompts saved twice, one restart notice per restart). 3 were served once and drawn twice. The two answer cases:
  - The October-plan tab (`muqqajso`, run `93052a5f`, 2026-10-02 22:52) drew the full answer, the full answer again, then the answer from raw offset 1130 to its end (`"…under 1 " | "m.\n- **Crowds:**"`).
  - The worker tab (`muqxwh64`, run `6a6ed93d`, 2026-10-03 05:16) drew the full answer, then the answer from raw offset 837 (`"never mad" | "e it out"`).
  - Both answers are a single claude-cli text block. The gateway logged `[chat-deliver]` twice per run with equal `textLen`, so two finals arrived with the same body.
  - On the October tab, a `chat.history` read landed between the two finals (22:52:25.837). The worker tab had none.
  - A capture of one real scratch turn (every frame plus `chat.history` every 6 s) showed deltas carrying the run's cumulative text, narration and answer glued with no separator, and the backstop final 0.9 s after the lifecycle one.
- **Root cause 1, the tail (`mixed-coordinates`):** the thinking writer stamps its bubbles with `_runId`, a text block and, since 2026-09-23 (`4e624e044ee`), a `_segmentStart` measured in the THINKING buffer. The second final selected the run's bubbles by `_runId` alone, so thoughts went into `resliceSegments` beside text bubbles.
  - Each bubble ends at the next one's start. The narration bubble before a thought therefore grew to `final[narrStart:thinkingOffset]`, over the head of the answer, inside a folded row.
  - `supersedingAppendTail` stopped its cursor there and returned `final.slice(thinkingOffset)`, which app.ts pushed as a new bubble.
  - The cut is a thinking length: 1364 in the worker case (`body[1364:]` is `answer[837:]`) and 2508 in the October case (`answer[1130:]`), recomputed from the claude-cli transcripts. The first final was clean only because it freezes reasoning bubbles before it selects temps.
- **Root cause 2, the second full copy (`chat-divergence`, cause 8 of the 2026-10-01 entry):** `history-reconcile.ts` closed a run's watched window 15 s after its newest live row OPENED (`historyRowTime` prefers `_bubbleStartedAt`). A claude-cli import row is stamped when its block FINISHED.
  - The October answer streamed for 16.2 s.
  - A `loadChat` answered under the live run was deferred (`pendingHistoryReload`), and final #1 released it.
  - The gap-fill then appended the served answer under the live one (`added 1`).
  - It does not need the race: any later history read (a tab switch-back, a reconnect) adds it on a run with no thinking. On thinking runs, develop's tail bubble widened the window by accident and hid it.
- **Root cause 3, attribution (`early-return-drops-attribute`):** `renderSectionedReply` returns early from `renderMsg`, before `ocIdAttrs`. A served answer with a 🌿 section therefore carried no `data-oc-id`, so the snapshot census and `scripts/ui-dup-proof.mjs` read the gap-filled twin as client-written. That made the October case look like two client writes.
- **Fix, first cut:**
  - `50e31500e5d`: `isRunTextBubble` excludes `_isReasoning`. Both finals select through `finalTextBubbles` and write through `planFinalWrite` (final-supersede.ts), replacing the inline `m._runId === p.runId` filter.
  - `8450820d539`: a new `watchedToOf` dependency extended a live row's coverage to `_bubbleEndedAt`.
  - `72ee1adb450`: `renderSectionedReply` takes `idAttrs(part)`, and both call sites pass `ocIdAttrs`.
- **Review of the first cut:** four lenses, and each finding was re-checked by an independent skeptic who tried to refute it. Five real regressions survived, all minor:
  - The window end read promotion time. A Stop end-stamps every leftover temp, so an hour-old frozen partial covered the hour and older pages lost rows another device had added. And a first final promotes a narration whose answer only the second final carries, so a tab left between the finals was never given that answer.
  - A prompt typed elsewhere while the answer streamed was hidden.
  - With thoughts out of the selection, a superseding final grew the last narration over an answer only it carried: one glued bubble. This geometry is real but rare: in 20 of 649 two-final runs from 2026-09-23 to 10-03 only the second final carried the answer.
  - A promptless run that showed only a thought took the no-temps path, where `unshownRunText` credited another run's identical answer and wrote nothing.
  - Stamped fold bodies (Commentary, 🌿) became the reload anchor, because Chromium reports a box for a closed fold's content, so a reload landed on the wrong row.
- **Fix, round 2:**
  - `b6e21a5b3bc`: a superseding final changes no settled bubble and appends what is new as its own bubble.
  - `0377b739228`: `runShownTexts` reads one run's rows, plus the frozen turn it resumes.
  - `ddaa32ebbd2`: the window extends to a CLOSED bubble's last write (`_lastWriteAt`, stamped by the live writers), never to a promotion, and a typed prompt is tested without the extension.
  - `388da2c8ffb`: only the answer and the deeper bubble carry the id.
  - `28d935b3ec1`: a source check pins app.ts's wiring.
  - Design criteria in tinker-ui.md §5.8V: "Two coordinate spaces", "Two finals per run", "What a run already shows is that run's", "Gap-fill window end".
- **Round 3 (`a43c73e39c0`):** a three-build check found one narrow case left. A promptless run that streamed only a thought, whose final equalled an earlier answer the page had loaded from history, credited that unstamped row and wrote nothing. Develop had drawn it only by the same accident as the tail. The scope now skips history rows stamped before the run started, known from its `lifecycle:start`.
- **Tests:**
  - Unit, each new case red before its round:
    - `final-supersede.test.ts`: "a reasoning bubble never takes part in a final's text reconciliation", on the worker case's measured geometry, and "app.ts wires the duplicate fixes", a source check.
    - `history-reconcile.test.ts`: "a live bubble covers its run until it was last written", on the October case's times.
    - `live-continuation.test.ts`: "scoped to one run", including the start floor.
    - `sectioned-reply.test.ts`: the visible parts.
    - Red counts: 7 failed on develop, 12 on the first cut, 1 on round 2. The whole tinker-ui suite passes (3177/3177), and the ad-hoc type check shows the same 643 errors as develop.
  - Committed real browser: `tinker-ui/e2e/chat-viewport/` check 12 (`4d6269f2bc6`, written as check 9 and renumbered when develop's own 9 to 11 merged in). Develop fails it: the answer twice plus a 700-character tail live, once after a reload. The fix passes it.
  - Replay harness (scratch, Chromium, mock gateway replaying captured frames with thinking added and answers stretched to case A's timing), comparing the live page with the reloaded one.
    - On the six reproductions of the two reported shapes, develop drew copies or tails in all six and round 3 in none: the live page equals the reload.
    - On the 30-odd scenarios the review built to break the fix, round 3 shows no answer or prompt row worse than develop, and three better.
    - Remaining live-versus-reload differences (Stop, reload mid-answer, the 419-row trim, a prompt from another device) are identical on develop.
- **Not fixed here:**
  - (1) Leaving a tab while its run is still going and coming back after it ended. The saved page keeps the bubble that was streaming, because background finals never write a saved page, and the run's window still ends 15 s after its newest live row OPENED.
    - A long answer's served row is merged in under the partial (a partial copy).
    - A short one's is skipped as watched, so the answer stays cut off.
    - Leaving during the last tool call can leave no answer at all.
    - Each was reproduced on develop and on the fix, and a reload shows the full answer.
    - A census of the 78 snapshots since 2026-09-23 with served history found at most one cut answer (`mul6nasy`, 2026-09-28). An answer missing outright leaves no bubble to count, so that census cannot see it.
    - The minimal fix the review proposed: when a tab is left, end its open runs' coverage at that moment, and continue the frozen bubble from the served row of the same block. It is a design change to the background path, and the owner's call.
  - (2) A history read that lands before final #1 adds the claude-cli import copy of the prompt below the client's prompt bubble, on every build: after a socket drop, and after a refresh when the turn started more than 15 s after the send (a cold start or a queued prompt). Until final #1 the gateway serves the prompt only as that import row (no `idempotencyKey`, stamped when the turn started), the page's prompt covers ±15 s around the send, and the run has no window before its first live row. From final #1 on the gateway serves the keyed row, so a later reload shows one prompt. Fixed for every prompt the gateway serves with its key by `[chat-divergence+scope-mismatch]` (2026-10-03); a new tab's first turn, served without one, is still open there.
  - (3) A served thinking row followed by a live copy of the same text (`muqqajso`, 2026-10-02 11:09, build `e13c040bad5`) was not reproduced.
  - (4) A narration split mid-word into two folded rows (`"I'll check the cl" | "ock…"`) is cosmetic: `streamMsgUid` is cleared without `lastDeltaLen`.
  - (5) Pre-existing, found by the review: the reload anchor walks (`captureViewportAnchor`, `captureScrollAnchor`, `restoreScrollAnchor`) treat height 0 as folded. Develop's plain-path Commentary body is stamped inside a closed fold, so the anchor can name it. Likely fix: skip `!checkVisibility()` nodes. Handed to the chat-viewport work, which fixed it in `f3aa928fe5a`.
  - (6) Develop's mid-run history writer (`6a5fff8871b`, merged into this branch before the merge to develop) writes the prompt's import copy on a switch-back under a live run. In the `cap1-fast-exercise-all` replay it also draws the run's text again from its first narration and drops a tool row; the old base draws neither. Found by this review and handed to the chat-viewport work that owns the writer (2026-10-03).
  - (3) appears only in the snapshot named; (2) and (4) were not counted. (Jarvis)

### FIXED [chat-divergence+scope-mismatch]: the prompt drawn twice after a reload under its run, or after its run finished in a background tab, when the turn started more than 15 s after the send (2026-10-03; bug id `prompt-twice-keyed-row`)

- **Symptom:** found by the review of `[mixed-coordinates+chat-divergence+early-return-drops-attribute]` (its completeness critic, gap 1). A refresh during a run drew the prompt twice, and a later reload showed it once. A refresh is what the owner does to clear a duplicate.
- **Measured (read-only, Tinker prompts from 2026-09-23 to 10-03):**
  - 173 of 375 reached the CLI more than 15 s after chat.send accepted them (median 13.6 s, p90 81.1 s). Nearly all of the wait is in the gateway before the model call (routing, tool wrapping, bootstrap reads), not in the CLI.
  - On an established tab the keyed local prompt row is written as the turn starts (510 of 511 before the CLI's copy), stamped then, and chat.history serves it from that moment.
  - On a new tab's first turn pi writes nothing until the turn's assistant row lands at final #1, so until then the prompt is served only as the claude-cli import row, with no key: 40 first prompts, a median window of 489 s, and a history read inside it on 9 of 23 typed first turns.
  - A cursor read dedups imports against its own slice of local rows only. For 13 prompts on established tabs the prompt's import twin was the newest served user row between the CLI row and final #1.
- **Root cause (`scope-mismatch`):** the bubble and the served row of one prompt share a key, and the page never compared it. send() mints one uuid for the bubble's `_clientMsgId` and for chat.send's `idempotencyKey`, and the gateway stamps it on the prompt row it serves. The history planners knew a served row only by `__openclaw.id` / `externalId` or by a time window, and `reinjectOutboxBubbles` counted a prompt as on screen only by `_clientMsgId`. Outside the bubble's ±15 s instant, and before the run had a live bubble:
  - a reload under the live run: `loadChat`'s live-run branch returns before `reconcileOutboxAgainst`, so nothing proves the entry, and the bubble the outbox puts back sits beside the served row, in either order;
  - a background tab: the refresh at the finals (`hydrateTab` → `mergeHistoryIntoSavedPage`) writes the served row beside the bubble its saved page kept.
- **Fix (`a66080a7962`):** `history-reconcile.ts historyPromptKey` and `promptKeysOf`, read through an optional `promptKeyOf` dependency. The tail merge, `planHistoryAroundLooseRows`, `planRowsByTime` and `planOlderPage` skip a served prompt whose key a page row holds, as they skip a row held by identity, and anchor on its holder. `reinjectOutboxBubbles` counts a served row's key as the entry's bubble on screen, which suppresses a bubble and never retires an entry. Identity only: another key, or the same text with no key, is drawn. Design criterion: tinker-ui.md §5.8V "A prompt is known by its key".
- **Tests:**
  - `prompt-key-identity.test.ts`: 15 tests on the replayed capture's times. 7 fail on develop's planners, run with the two new functions added to develop so the failures are the behaviour, not a missing import.
  - Real browser: `tinker-ui/e2e/chat-viewport/` check 13 (`817bc33c0d7`), a turn that starts 20 s after the send: (a) a reload after its start and before its first thought, (b) the tab away until both finals. Develop `0191e371542` drew the prompt twice after the reload, still twice after the finals, twice back from away, and once after a fresh reload. The fix draws it once everywhere. Checks 1-12 pass on both.
  - Replay harness: 24 prompt scenarios from the client investigation, real Chromium. Where the served prompt carries the page's key, develop drew it twice in 9 scenarios (a reload under the run, a switch-back before the run's first live bubble, a background tab through the finals, on new and established tabs) and the fix draws it once in all 9. Where it carries none (a new tab's first turn), both draw it twice (9 scenarios). 6 were single on both. The fix never draws more prompts than develop. The 47-scenario regression list shows no new live-versus-reload difference.
- **Not fixed here:**
  - (1) A prompt the gateway serves with NO key: a new tab's first turn until final #1, and a cursor read's import twin. The page has nothing to compare, so a reload in a new tab's first turn still draws the prompt twice. Both need the gateway. For the first turn the choices are to write a cold transcript at its first prompt row (`session-manager-init.ts`; the keyed row then exists from the turn's start, as on an established tab, but it changes when every new session's first turn is persisted), or to key the import row at read time from the session's active run. For the twin, a cursor read's imports would be deduped against the local rows before its anchor too.
  - (2) An import copy of a prompt shows the gateway's chat-row contract (`<!-- TINKERCLAW chat-row contract --> Before EVERY tool call in your response…`) as text under the owner's words: 1457 rendered characters where the keyed row shows 824. Only an import copy carries it, so it shows exactly where (1) does.
  - (3) After a refresh that lands before final #1, the run's deltas can reach the page before its delayed history read. With nothing on the page to credit, the live writer starts one bubble from offset 0, final #1 promotes it, and the read then writes the served rows around it: both narrations glued to the answer (2989 characters against the served 2895), the first narration also in its folded row, the second thought and a tool row missing (`cap1-fast-reload-midanswer`, `cap1-fast-exercise-all`; old base, develop and this fix alike, depending on when the read lands). The 2026-09-23 live continuation handles history first and deltas second, not the reverse.
